3183	    _geo_sql = text(
3184	        f"""
3185	        WITH {geo_pro_cte}base AS (
3186	        SELECT p.idu, p.surface_m2,
3187	               ST_AsGeoJSON(ST_SimplifyPreserveTopology(p.geom, 0.00002)) AS g,
3188	               s2.tier AS tier_v2, s2.rang AS rang_v2, s2.mult_base AS mult_v2,
3189	               s2.p_raw AS p_raw, s2.top5_contributions AS top5,   -- M135 : fraction + raison (carte)
3190	               s2.copro AS copro_v2, s2.event_date,
3191	               (vw.parcelle_id IS NOT NULL) AS veille,
3192	               (d.status IN ('exclue', 'faux_positif_probable')) AS etage0,
3193	               d.status AS status, d.opportunity_score,  -- M129-B : matrice morte → statut cascade
3194	               d.completeness_score, r.sdp_residuelle_m2, r.sous_densite, r.taux_emprise_pct,
3195	               {geo_pro_col} AS permis_pro,     -- RECETTE-3 (T4) : « un pro a déposé un permis »
3196	               (ev.parcel_id IS NOT NULL) AS evenement_rouge, fl.flags,
3197	               cl.n AS cluster, COALESCE(cl.denom, own.denomination) AS proprio,
3198	               vs.v_score, vs.v_band, vs.owner_type,
3199	               -- CRED-4 : fraîcheur du signal V daté le plus récent (BODACC/cessation/DPE)
3200	               (SELECT max(s1->>'date_evenement') FROM jsonb_array_elements(vs.signals) s1
3201	                 WHERE s1->>'date_evenement' IS NOT NULL)    AS v_dernier_signal,
3202	               (SELECT array_agg(s0->>'code') FROM jsonb_array_elements(vs.signals) s0) AS v_sig
3203	               {ban_cols}{zone_cols}
3204	        FROM parcels p
3205	        {ban_join}
3206	        {zone_join}
3207	        JOIN dryrun_parcel_evaluations d ON d.parcel_id = p.id AND d.run_label = :run
3208	        LEFT JOIN parcel_p_score_v2 s2 ON s2.parcelle_id = p.idu AND s2.run_id = :v2run
3209	        LEFT JOIN parcel_veille_succession vw ON vw.parcelle_id = p.idu
3210	        LEFT JOIN parcel_v_score vs ON vs.parcelle_id = p.idu
3211	        LEFT JOIN parcelle_personne_morale own ON own.idu = p.idu
3212	        LEFT JOIN (SELECT pm2.siren, count(*) AS n, max(pm2.denomination) AS denom
3213	                   FROM dryrun_parcel_evaluations d2
3214	                   JOIN parcels p2 ON p2.id = d2.parcel_id
3215	                   JOIN parcel_p_score_v2 s22 ON s22.parcelle_id = p2.idu AND s22.run_id = :v2run
3216	                   JOIN parcelle_personne_morale pm2 ON pm2.idu = p2.idu
3217	                   WHERE d2.run_label = :run AND s22.tier IN ('brulante', 'chaude', 'priorite', 'a_suivre')
3218	                     AND NOT (d2.status IN ('exclue', 'faux_positif_probable'))
3219	                     AND pm2.siren IS NOT NULL
3220	                   GROUP BY pm2.siren HAVING count(*) > 1) cl ON cl.siren = own.siren
3221	        LEFT JOIN parcel_residuel r ON r.parcel_id = p.id AND r.cause IS NULL
3222	        {geo_pro_join}
3223	        LEFT JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT parcel_id FROM dryrun_cascade_results
3224	                   WHERE run_label = :run AND evenement = 'rouge') ev ON ev.parcel_id = p.id
3225	        -- flags actifs par parcelle (filtres métier) : couches en SOFT_FLAG + ABF non instruit.
3226	        -- M6.2 perf (#1) : SCOPÉ à la commune (join parcels) — sinon cette agrégation scanne les
3227	        -- 14 M lignes de dryrun_cascade_results à l'île entière (~5,4 s FIXE) à CHAQUE requête
3228	        -- commune. Les flags sont PAR PARCELLE (indépendants entre parcelles) → scoper est
3229	        -- sémantiquement identique. Commune NULL (mode île, rare en geojson) = comportement inchangé.
3230	        LEFT JOIN (SELECT cr.parcel_id, array_agg(DISTINCT cr.layer_name) AS flags
3231	                   FROM dryrun_cascade_results cr
3232	                   JOIN parcels pf ON pf.id = cr.parcel_id
3233	                        AND (CAST(:c AS text) IS NULL OR pf.commune = :c)
3234	                   WHERE cr.run_label = :run AND (cr.result = 'SOFT_FLAG'
3235	                         OR (cr.layer_name = 'abf' AND cr.result = 'UNKNOWN'))
3236	                   GROUP BY cr.parcel_id) fl ON fl.parcel_id = p.id
3237	        WHERE (CAST(:c AS text) IS NULL OR p.commune = :c)
3238	          AND (p.surface_m2 IS NULL OR p.surface_m2 >= :minsurf)
3239	        LIMIT :lim
3240	        )
3241	        SELECT json_build_object('type', 'FeatureCollection', 'features',
3242	          coalesce(json_agg(json_build_object(
3243	            'type', 'Feature',
3244	            'geometry', b.g::json,
3245	            'properties', json_build_object(
3246	              'idu', b.idu,
3247	              'surface_m2', CASE WHEN b.surface_m2 IS NULL OR b.surface_m2 = 0 THEN NULL
3248	                                 ELSE round(b.surface_m2)::int END,
3249	              'adresse', CASE WHEN b.ban_voie IS NULL OR b.ban_voie = '' THEN NULL
3250	                              ELSE b.ban_voie || CASE
3251	                                WHEN NULLIF(concat_ws(' ', b.ban_cp, b.ban_commune), '') IS NOT NULL
3252	                                THEN ', ' || concat_ws(' ', b.ban_cp, b.ban_commune) ELSE '' END END,
3253	              'status', b.status,
3254	              'tier_v2', b.tier_v2, 'rang_v2', b.rang_v2, 'mult_v2', b.mult_v2,
3255	              'fraction', {_frac_case}, 'top5', b.top5,   -- M135 : fraction (SQL) + top5 (raison au front)
3256	              'etage0', b.etage0,
3257	              -- M129-B : q_score/a_score/a_completude retirés (matrice morte)
3258	              'completeness_score', b.completeness_score,
3259	              'sdp_residuelle_m2', b.sdp_residuelle_m2, 'sous_densite', b.sous_densite,
3260	              -- RECETTE-3 (T4) : état du bien + signal « pro a déposé un permis » (segmentation client)
3261	              'etat_bien', CASE WHEN COALESCE(b.taux_emprise_pct, 0) < 5 THEN 'nu'
3262	                                WHEN COALESCE(b.sdp_residuelle_m2, 0) > 0 THEN 'bati_encore'
3263	                                ELSE 'bati_max' END,
3264	              'permis_pro', b.permis_pro,
3265	              'evenement', CASE WHEN b.evenement_rouge THEN 'rouge' ELSE NULL END,
3266	              'evenement_date', CASE WHEN b.event_date IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE b.event_date::text END,

68	INSERT INTO parcel_viabilisation
69	  (idu, commune, zone_fam, c100, c200, c100_recent, c100_acheve,
70	   voie10, voie75, bati10, bati30, bati75, assainissement_zonage, score, band)

72	       voie10, voie75, bati10, bati30, bati75, assainissement_zonage,

78	    (SELECT count(*) FROM via_permits_geo w WHERE ST_DWithin(p.geom_2975,w.g,200)) c200,
79	    (SELECT count(*) FROM via_permits_geo w WHERE ST_DWithin(p.geom_2975,w.g,100) AND w.d>=make_date(:annee,1,1)) c100_recent,
80	    (SELECT count(*) FROM via_permits_geo w WHERE ST_DWithin(p.geom_2975,w.g,100) AND w.etat='6') c100_acheve,
81	    EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM spatial_layers v WHERE v.kind='voirie' AND v.commune=p.commune AND ST_DWithin(p.geom_2975,v.geom_2975,10)) voie10,
82	    EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM spatial_layers v WHERE v.kind='voirie' AND v.commune=p.commune AND ST_DWithin(p.geom_2975,v.geom_2975,75)) voie75,
83	    EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM spatial_layers b WHERE b.kind='batiment' AND b.commune=p.commune AND ST_DWithin(p.geom_2975,b.geom_2975,10)) bati10,
84	    EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM spatial_layers b WHERE b.kind='batiment' AND b.commune=p.commune AND ST_DWithin(p.geom_2975,b.geom_2975,30)) bati30,
85	    EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM spatial_layers b WHERE b.kind='batiment' AND b.commune=p.commune AND ST_DWithin(p.geom_2975,b.geom_2975,75)) bati75,
86	    (SELECT za.subtype FROM spatial_layers za WHERE za.kind='zonage_assainissement'
87	       AND za.commune=p.commune AND ST_Intersects(p.geom_2975, za.geom_2975)
88	       ORDER BY CASE za.subtype WHEN 'collectif' THEN 0 ELSE 1 END LIMIT 1) assainissement_zonage

92	) s

64	# Parts par surface d'intersection — uniquement quand la sonde voit > 1 zone.
65	_PARTS = text(f"""
66	WITH p AS (SELECT geom_2975 AS g, ST_Area(geom_2975) AS a FROM parcels WHERE id = :pid)
67	SELECT {_TOK} AS zone, {_FAM} AS fam,
68	       round((100 * sum(ST_Area(ST_Intersection(ST_MakeValid(z.geom_2975), p.g)))
69	              / NULLIF((SELECT a FROM p), 0))::numeric, 1) AS pct
70	FROM spatial_layers z, p
71	WHERE z.kind = 'plu_gpu_zone' AND ST_Intersects(z.geom_2975, p.g)
72	GROUP BY 1, 2 HAVING sum(ST_Area(ST_Intersection(ST_MakeValid(z.geom_2975), p.g))) > 0
73	ORDER BY pct DESC
74	""")

--------------------------------------------------
>> Issue: [B608:hardcoded_sql_expressions] Possible SQL injection vector through string-based query construction.
   Severity: Medium   Confidence: Low
   CWE: CWE-89 (https://cwe.mitre.org/data/definitions/89.html)
   More Info: https://bandit.readthedocs.io/en/1.9.4/plugins/b608_hardcoded_sql_expressions.html
   Location: src/labuse/renouvellement.py:165:13
164	
165	_BUILD_SQL = """
166	WITH bati_x AS (
167	    SELECT DISTINCT ON (p.idu) p.idu, {code} AS code
168	    FROM dryrun_cascade_results cr
169	    JOIN parcels p ON p.id = cr.parcel_id
170	    WHERE cr.run_label = :cascade AND cr.layer_name = 'bati'
171	      AND cr.result = 'SOFT_FLAG' AND ({code}) IS NOT NULL
172	    ORDER BY p.idu, cr.id DESC
173	),
174	public_x AS (
175	    SELECT DISTINCT p.idu
176	    FROM dryrun_cascade_results cr
177	    JOIN parcels p ON p.id = cr.parcel_id
178	    WHERE cr.run_label = :cascade AND cr.layer_name = 'foncier_public'
179	      AND cr.result = 'SOFT_FLAG'
180	),
181	-- RETOURS-11F M9 — contraintes NON constructibles servies par le run (jamais inventées) :
182	-- PPR zone rouge (fraction RÉELLE lue dans le motif « N % de la surface »), ravine (contact
183	-- berge), mouvement de terrain. Elles réduisent la SDP résiduelle exploitable → la NETTE.
184	ppr_x AS (
185	    SELECT p.idu,
186	           max((regexp_match(cr.detail, '([0-9]+) % de la surface'))[1]::int) AS pct_rouge
187	    FROM dryrun_cascade_results cr
188	    JOIN parcels p ON p.id = cr.parcel_id
189	    WHERE cr.run_label = :cascade AND cr.layer_name = 'risques'
190	      AND cr.result = 'HARD_EXCLUDE' AND cr.detail LIKE '%PPR zone rouge%'
191	    GROUP BY p.idu
192	),
193	ravine_x AS (
194	    SELECT DISTINCT p.idu FROM dryrun_cascade_results cr JOIN parcels p ON p.id = cr.parcel_id
195	    WHERE cr.run_label = :cascade AND cr.layer_name = 'ravine' AND cr.result = 'SOFT_FLAG'
196	),
197	mvt_x AS (
198	    SELECT DISTINCT p.idu FROM dryrun_cascade_results cr JOIN parcels p ON p.id = cr.parcel_id
199	    WHERE cr.run_label = :cascade AND cr.layer_name = 'mvt' AND cr.result = 'SOFT_FLAG'
200	),
201	seg AS (
202	    SELECT b.idu, b.code, left(b.idu, 5) AS commune, d.zone_plu,
203	           coalesce(d.sdp_residuelle_m2, 0)::int AS sdp,
204	           round(d.surface_m2)::int              AS surface,
205	           coalesce(d.rot_bati_brute, 0)         AS rot_bati,
206	           -- facteur de constructibilité MULTIPLICATIF borné [0,1] (config capacite_nette) :
207	           greatest(0.0, (
208	               (1.0 - coalesce(ppr.pct_rouge, 0) / 100.0)
209	             * CASE WHEN tan(radians(coalesce(t.pente_non_batie_deg, t.pente_moy_deg, 0))) * 100
210	                         > :pente_seuil THEN :f_pente ELSE 1.0 END
211	             * CASE WHEN rav.idu IS NOT NULL THEN :f_ravine ELSE 1.0 END
212	             * CASE WHEN mvt.idu IS NOT NULL THEN :f_mvt   ELSE 1.0 END
213	           )) AS frac_net
214	           -- OUTILS-FIX-1 C1 (décision Vic 06/09) : la surélévation N'EST PLUS calculée ici. EXPORTS-1
215	           -- lot 3 avait débranché la table orpheline (parcel_residuel_bati, faîtage) en écrivant
216	           -- false/NULL en dur ; la colonne servie restait donc périmée jusqu'au prochain rebuild
217	           -- (VERIF-1 Q3). On cesse d'alimenter surelevable/niveaux_sur : « pas de signal plutôt qu'un
218	           -- signal périmé ». Le signal VIVANT reste au moteur commun (faisabilite/potentiel.surelevation,
219	           -- hauteur à l'égout), servi par la fiche. Les colonnes de parcel_renouvellement seront
220	           -- supprimées au prochain rebuild de schéma (pas de migration ici).
221	    FROM bati_x b
222	    JOIN p_model_ext_dataset d ON d.idu = b.idu AND d.annee = :annee
223	    LEFT JOIN p_model_ext_copro c ON c.idu = b.idu
224	    LEFT JOIN ppr_x    ppr ON ppr.idu = b.idu
225	    LEFT JOIN ravine_x rav ON rav.idu = b.idu
226	    LEFT JOIN mvt_x    mvt ON mvt.idu = b.idu
227	    LEFT JOIN parcel_terrain      t  ON t.idu  = b.idu
228	    WHERE d.zone_plu IN ('U', 'AU')
229	      AND (d.sdp_residuelle_m2 > :sdp_min OR d.surface_m2 >= :surf_min)
230	      AND NOT coalesce(c.copro_rnic OR c.copro_dvf, false)
231	      AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public_x px WHERE px.idu = b.idu)
232	),
233	netd AS (
234	    -- SDP NETTE = SDP brute × facteur de constructibilité ; part de contrainte déduite (0-100).
235	    SELECT s.*,
236	           round(s.sdp * s.frac_net)::int                  AS sdp_nette,
237	           round(100 * (1.0 - s.frac_net))::int            AS contrainte_pct
238	    FROM seg s
239	),
240	comp AS (
241	    SELECT n.*,
242	           -- percent_rank BRUTS (0-1) — la source de discrimination fine ; les composantes affichées
243	           -- (points arrondis) restent des ENTIERS pour le « pourquoi », mais ne portent PLUS le score.
244	           -- M9 : le potentiel rank sur la SDP NETTE (contraintes déduites), plus sur la brute.
245	           :w_pot * percent_rank() OVER (ORDER BY n.sdp_nette)      AS pr_pot,
246	           :w_ass * percent_rank() OVER (ORDER BY n.surface)        AS pr_ass,
247	           :w_mar * percent_rank() OVER (ORDER BY n.rot_bati)       AS pr_mar,
248	           round(:w_pot * percent_rank() OVER (ORDER BY n.sdp_nette))::int    AS comp_potentiel,
249	           round(:w_ass * percent_rank() OVER (ORDER BY n.surface))::int      AS comp_assiette,
250	           round(:w_mar * percent_rank() OVER (ORDER BY n.rot_bati))::int     AS comp_marche
251	    FROM netd n
252	),
253	scored AS (
254	    -- RETOURS-11F M9 — score CONTINU (1 décimale) issu des percent_rank bruts : plus de plateau à 100.
255	    -- Les poids somment à 100 (config) → percent_rank ≤ 1 borne à 100 sans clamp (LEAST retiré : il ne
256	    -- servait qu'à masquer un dépassement impossible ici, et cachait la vérité du score en tête).
257	    SELECT c.*,
258	           round((c.pr_pot + c.pr_ass + c.pr_mar)::numeric, 1) AS score
259	    FROM comp c
260	)
261	INSERT INTO parcel_renouvellement
262	    (idu, renouv_score, comp_potentiel, comp_assiette, comp_marche,
263	     code_bati_origine, sdp_residuelle_m2, sdp_nette_m2, contrainte_pct,
264	     surface_m2, zone_plu, commune,
265	     rang_segment, rang_commune, run_label)
266	SELECT idu, score, comp_potentiel, comp_assiette, comp_marche,
267	       code, nullif(sdp, 0), nullif(sdp_nette, 0), contrainte_pct,
268	       surface, zone_plu, commune,
269	       rank() OVER (ORDER BY score DESC, idu),
270	       rank() OVER (PARTITION BY commune ORDER BY score DESC, idu),
271	       :run
272	FROM scored
273	""".replace("{code}", _CODE_CASE)
274

   Severity: Medium   Confidence: Low

   More Info: https://bandit.readthedocs.io/en/1.9.4/plugins/b608_hardcoded_sql_expressions.html
   Location: src/labuse/renouvellement.py:332:10
331	        .replace("{base}", _BATI_BASE)
332	        + " AND NOT coalesce((SELECT c.copro_rnic OR c.copro_dvf FROM p_model_ext_copro c"
333	          "                   WHERE c.idu = p.idu), false)"), params).scalar() or 0)
334	    funnel["4_hors_copro"] = sans_copro
335	    funnel["5_hors_foncier_public_final"] = n

159	    _exec(session, f"""
160	        DROP TABLE IF EXISTS p_model_ext_dataset;
161	        CREATE TABLE p_model_ext_dataset AS
162	        WITH years(annee) AS (VALUES {yrs}),
163	        maxy AS (
164	            SELECT coalesce(extract(year FROM max(date_mutation))::int, 0) AS y
165	            FROM p_model_ext_mut_l2
166	        ),
167	        stock AS (
168	            SELECT secteur, count(*)::float AS n_parcelles FROM p_model_frame GROUP BY 1
169	        ),
170	        win AS (
171	            SELECT annee,
172	                   make_date(annee, 1, 1)                                AS asof,
173	                   greatest(make_date(annee - 3, 1, 1), {EXT_DVF_START}) AS w36_start,
174	                   make_date(annee - 1, 1, 1)                            AS w12_start,
175	                   greatest(make_date(annee - 2, 1, 1), {EXT_DVF_START}) AS w24_start
176	            FROM years
177	        ),
178	        z_sect AS (
179	            SELECT f.secteur, w.annee,
180	                   count(DISTINCT m.id_mutation) FILTER (WHERE NOT m.bati AND NOT m.exclue_l2f) AS n_mut_nu_36m,
181	                   count(DISTINCT m.id_mutation) FILTER (WHERE m.bati AND NOT m.exclue_l2f)     AS n_mut_bati_36m,
182	                   percentile_cont(0.5) WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY m.pm2_terrain)
183	                       FILTER (WHERE m.pm2_terrain IS NOT NULL)            AS med_pm2_terrain_36m,
184	                   percentile_cont(0.5) WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY m.pm2_bati)
185	                       FILTER (WHERE m.pm2_bati IS NOT NULL)               AS med_pm2_bati_36m,
186	                   percentile_cont(0.5) WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY m.pm2_bati)
187	                       FILTER (WHERE m.pm2_bati IS NOT NULL
188	                               AND m.date_mutation >= w.w12_start)         AS med_pm2_bati_12m,
189	                   percentile_cont(0.5) WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY m.pm2_bati)
190	                       FILTER (WHERE m.pm2_bati IS NOT NULL
191	                               AND m.date_mutation < w.w12_start)          AS med_pm2_bati_avant
192	            FROM p_model_ext_mut_l2 m
193	            JOIN p_model_frame f ON f.idu = m.idu
194	            CROSS JOIN win w
195	            WHERE m.date_mutation >= w.w36_start AND m.date_mutation < w.asof
196	            GROUP BY 1, 2
197	        ),
198	        z_perm AS (
199	            SELECT f.secteur, w.annee, count(DISTINCT pp.permit_id) AS n_permis_24m
200	            FROM p_model_permits pp
201	            JOIN p_model_frame f ON f.idu = pp.idu
202	            CROSS JOIN win w
203	            WHERE pp.type IN ('PC', 'PA')
204	              AND pp.date_autorisation >= w.w24_start AND pp.date_autorisation < w.asof
205	            GROUP BY 1, 2
206	        ),
207	        d_tenure AS (
208	            SELECT ma.idu, w.annee, max(ma.date_mutation) AS derniere_mutation
209	            FROM p_model_ext_mut_all ma CROSS JOIN win w
210	            WHERE ma.date_mutation < w.asof GROUP BY 1, 2
211	        ),
212	        d_permit AS (
213	            SELECT pp.idu, w.annee, max(pp.date_autorisation) AS dernier_permis
214	            FROM p_model_permits pp CROSS JOIN win w
215	            WHERE pp.date_autorisation < w.asof GROUP BY 1, 2
216	        ),
217	        dens AS (
218	            SELECT f.secteur,
219	                   sum(coalesce(st.emprise_bati_m2, 0)) / nullif(sum(f.surface_m2), 0) AS dens_bati_secteur,
220	                   avg((coalesce(st.emprise_bati_m2, 0) > 20)::int)::float             AS pct_bati_secteur
221	            FROM p_model_frame f JOIN p_model_static st ON st.idu = f.idu GROUP BY 1
222	        )
223	        SELECT
224	            f.idu, w.annee,
225	            CASE WHEN w.annee <= (SELECT y FROM maxy) THEN
226	                 (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM p_model_ext_mut_l2 mu
227	                          WHERE mu.idu = f.idu AND NOT mu.exclue_l2f
228	                            AND mu.date_mutation >= w.asof
229	                            AND mu.date_mutation < make_date(w.annee + 1, 1, 1)))::int
230	            END AS label,
231	            CASE WHEN w.annee <= (SELECT y FROM maxy) THEN
232	                 (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM p_model_ext_mut_l2 mu
233	                          WHERE mu.idu = f.idu
234	                            AND mu.date_mutation >= w.asof
235	                            AND mu.date_mutation < make_date(w.annee + 1, 1, 1)))::int
236	            END AS label_l2,
237	            f.commune, f.secteur, st.owner_type,
238	            least((w.asof - w.w36_start) / 30.44, 36.0) / 36.0                  AS window_coverage,
239	            coalesce(zs.n_mut_nu_36m, 0)                                        AS n_mut_nu_36m,
240	            coalesce(zs.n_mut_bati_36m, 0)                                      AS n_mut_bati_36m,
241	            sk.n_parcelles                                                      AS stock_secteur,
242	            coalesce(zs.n_mut_nu_36m, 0)   / sk.n_parcelles
243	                / ((w.asof - w.w36_start) / 30.44) * 12                         AS rot_nu_brute,
244	            coalesce(zs.n_mut_bati_36m, 0) / sk.n_parcelles
245	                / ((w.asof - w.w36_start) / 30.44) * 12                         AS rot_bati_brute,
246	            zs.med_pm2_terrain_36m, zs.med_pm2_bati_36m,
247	            CASE WHEN zs.med_pm2_bati_avant > 0
248	                 THEN zs.med_pm2_bati_12m / zs.med_pm2_bati_avant - 1 END       AS tendance_pm2_bati,
249	            coalesce(zp.n_permis_24m, 0) / sk.n_parcelles                       AS permis_24m_norm,
250	            dn.dens_bati_secteur, dn.pct_bati_secteur,
251	            st.filo_snv_pp, st.filo_pct_pauv, st.filo_pct_prop, st.filo_dens_pop,
252	            st.qpv, st.pente_moy_deg,
253	            st.dist_ecole_m, st.dist_sante_m, st.dist_commerce_m, st.dist_tcsp_m,
254	            coalesce(st.zone_plu, 'inconnu')                                    AS zone_plu,
255	            (st.emprise_bati_m2 <= 20)                                          AS nu,
256	            (st.emprise_bati_m2 <= 20
257	             AND coalesce(st.zone_plu, '') IN ('U', 'AU'))                      AS nu_constructible,
258	            f.surface_m2, st.emprise_bati_m2,
259	            st.pct_potentiel, st.sous_densite, st.sdp_residuelle_m2,
260	            CASE
261	                WHEN dt.derniere_mutation IS NULL THEN 'inconnu'
262	                WHEN dt.derniere_mutation >= w.asof - INTERVAL '1 year'  THEN '<1'
263	                WHEN dt.derniere_mutation >= w.asof - INTERVAL '2 years' THEN '1-2'
264	                WHEN dt.derniere_mutation >= w.asof - INTERVAL '3 years' THEN '2-3'
265	                ELSE '3+'
266	            END AS tenure_bin,
267	            CASE
268	                WHEN dp.dernier_permis IS NULL THEN 'jamais'
269	                WHEN dp.dernier_permis >= w.asof - INTERVAL '2 years'  THEN '<2a'
270	                WHEN dp.dernier_permis >= w.asof - INTERVAL '5 years'  THEN '2-5a'
271	                WHEN dp.dernier_permis >= w.asof - INTERVAL '10 years' THEN '5-10a'
272	                ELSE '10a+'
273	            END AS permis_bin,
274	            st.canopee_pct, st.ndvi_moyen, st.friche, st.piscine, st.pv_candidat
275	        FROM p_model_frame f
276	        CROSS JOIN win w
277	        JOIN stock sk ON sk.secteur = f.secteur
278	        JOIN p_model_static st ON st.idu = f.idu
279	        LEFT JOIN z_sect zs ON zs.secteur = f.secteur AND zs.annee = w.annee
280	        LEFT JOIN z_perm zp ON zp.secteur = f.secteur AND zp.annee = w.annee
281	        LEFT JOIN d_tenure dt ON dt.idu = f.idu AND dt.annee = w.annee
282	        LEFT JOIN d_permit dp ON dp.idu = f.idu AND dp.annee = w.annee
283	        LEFT JOIN dens dn ON dn.secteur = f.secteur;
284	        CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ON p_model_ext_dataset (idu, annee);
285	        CREATE INDEX ON p_model_ext_dataset (annee);
286	    """)

395	    _exec(session, f"""
396	        DROP TABLE IF EXISTS p_model_dataset;
397	        CREATE TABLE p_model_dataset AS
398	        WITH years(annee) AS (VALUES {yrs}),
399	        maxy AS (
400	            SELECT coalesce(extract(year FROM max(date_mutation))::int, 0) AS y
401	            FROM p_model_mut_l2
402	        ),
403	        stock AS (
404	            SELECT secteur, count(*)::float AS n_parcelles
405	            FROM p_model_frame GROUP BY 1
406	        ),
407	        win AS (
408	            SELECT annee,
409	                   make_date(annee, 1, 1)                            AS asof,
410	                   greatest(make_date(annee - 3, 1, 1), {DVF_START}) AS w36_start,
411	                   make_date(annee - 1, 1, 1)                        AS w12_start,
412	                   greatest(make_date(annee - 2, 1, 1), {DVF_START}) AS w24_start
413	            FROM years
414	        ),
415	        -- Z : événements L2 STRICTEMENT antérieurs au 01/01/Y, par secteur × année
416	        z_sect AS (
417	            SELECT f.secteur, w.annee,
418	                   count(DISTINCT m.id_mutation) FILTER (WHERE NOT m.bati) AS n_mut_nu_36m,
419	                   count(DISTINCT m.id_mutation) FILTER (WHERE m.bati)     AS n_mut_bati_36m,
420	                   percentile_cont(0.5) WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY m.pm2_terrain)
421	                       FILTER (WHERE m.pm2_terrain IS NOT NULL)            AS med_pm2_terrain_36m,
422	                   percentile_cont(0.5) WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY m.pm2_bati)
423	                       FILTER (WHERE m.pm2_bati IS NOT NULL)               AS med_pm2_bati_36m,
424	                   percentile_cont(0.5) WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY m.pm2_bati)
425	                       FILTER (WHERE m.pm2_bati IS NOT NULL
426	                               AND m.date_mutation >= w.w12_start)         AS med_pm2_bati_12m,
427	                   percentile_cont(0.5) WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY m.pm2_bati)
428	                       FILTER (WHERE m.pm2_bati IS NOT NULL
429	                               AND m.date_mutation < w.w12_start)          AS med_pm2_bati_avant
430	            FROM p_model_mut_l2 m
431	            JOIN p_model_frame f ON f.idu = m.idu
432	            CROSS JOIN win w
433	            WHERE m.date_mutation >= w.w36_start AND m.date_mutation < w.asof
434	            GROUP BY 1, 2
435	        ),
436	        z_perm AS (
437	            SELECT f.secteur, w.annee, count(DISTINCT pp.permit_id) AS n_permis_24m
438	            FROM p_model_permits pp
439	            JOIN p_model_frame f ON f.idu = pp.idu
440	            CROSS JOIN win w
441	            WHERE pp.type IN ('PC', 'PA')
442	              AND pp.date_autorisation >= w.w24_start AND pp.date_autorisation < w.asof
443	            GROUP BY 1, 2
444	        ),
445	        d_tenure AS (
446	            SELECT ma.idu, w.annee, max(ma.date_mutation) AS derniere_mutation
447	            FROM p_model_mut_all ma
448	            CROSS JOIN win w
449	            WHERE ma.date_mutation < w.asof
450	            GROUP BY 1, 2
451	        ),
452	        d_permit AS (
453	            SELECT pp.idu, w.annee, max(pp.date_autorisation) AS dernier_permis
454	            FROM p_model_permits pp
455	            CROSS JOIN win w
456	            WHERE pp.date_autorisation < w.asof
457	            GROUP BY 1, 2
458	        ),
459	        dens AS (
460	            SELECT f.secteur,
461	                   sum(coalesce(st.emprise_bati_m2, 0)) / nullif(sum(f.surface_m2), 0) AS dens_bati_secteur,
462	                   avg((coalesce(st.emprise_bati_m2, 0) > {NU_SEUIL_M2})::int)::float  AS pct_bati_secteur
463	            FROM p_model_frame f JOIN p_model_static st ON st.idu = f.idu
464	            GROUP BY 1
465	        )
466	        SELECT
467	            f.idu, w.annee,
468	            -- ===== label : mutation L2 dans [01/01/Y, 31/12/Y] =====
469	            CASE WHEN w.annee <= (SELECT y FROM maxy) THEN
470	                 (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM p_model_mut_l2 mu
471	                          WHERE mu.idu = f.idu
472	                            AND mu.date_mutation >= w.asof
473	                            AND mu.date_mutation < make_date(w.annee + 1, 1, 1)))::int
474	            END AS label,
475	            -- ===== méta (JAMAIS features) =====
476	            f.commune, f.secteur, st.owner_type,
477	            -- ===== bloc Z =====
478	            least((w.asof - w.w36_start) / 30.44, 36.0) / 36.0                  AS window_coverage,
479	            coalesce(zs.n_mut_nu_36m, 0)                                        AS n_mut_nu_36m,
480	            coalesce(zs.n_mut_bati_36m, 0)                                      AS n_mut_bati_36m,
481	            sk.n_parcelles                                                      AS stock_secteur,
482	            coalesce(zs.n_mut_nu_36m, 0)   / sk.n_parcelles
483	                / ((w.asof - w.w36_start) / 30.44) * 12                         AS rot_nu_brute,
484	            coalesce(zs.n_mut_bati_36m, 0) / sk.n_parcelles
485	                / ((w.asof - w.w36_start) / 30.44) * 12                         AS rot_bati_brute,
486	            zs.med_pm2_terrain_36m, zs.med_pm2_bati_36m,
487	            CASE WHEN zs.med_pm2_bati_avant > 0
488	                 THEN zs.med_pm2_bati_12m / zs.med_pm2_bati_avant - 1 END       AS tendance_pm2_bati,
489	            coalesce(zp.n_permis_24m, 0) / sk.n_parcelles                       AS permis_24m_norm,
490	            dn.dens_bati_secteur, dn.pct_bati_secteur,
491	            st.filo_snv_pp, st.filo_pct_pauv, st.filo_pct_prop, st.filo_dens_pop,
492	            st.qpv, st.pente_moy_deg,
493	            st.dist_ecole_m, st.dist_sante_m, st.dist_commerce_m, st.dist_tcsp_m,
494	            coalesce(st.zone_plu, 'inconnu')                                    AS zone_plu,
495	            -- ===== bloc D =====
496	            (st.emprise_bati_m2 <= {NU_SEUIL_M2})                               AS nu,
497	            (st.emprise_bati_m2 <= {NU_SEUIL_M2}
498	             AND coalesce(st.zone_plu, '') IN ('U', 'AU'))                      AS nu_constructible,
499	            f.surface_m2,
500	            st.emprise_bati_m2,
501	            st.pct_potentiel, st.sous_densite, st.sdp_residuelle_m2,
502	            CASE
503	                WHEN dt.derniere_mutation IS NULL THEN 'inconnu'
504	                WHEN dt.derniere_mutation >= w.asof - INTERVAL '1 year'  THEN '<1'
505	                WHEN dt.derniere_mutation >= w.asof - INTERVAL '2 years' THEN '1-2'
506	                WHEN dt.derniere_mutation >= w.asof - INTERVAL '3 years' THEN '2-3'
507	                ELSE '3+'
508	            END                                                                 AS tenure_bin,
509	            CASE
510	                WHEN dp.dernier_permis IS NULL THEN 'jamais'
511	                WHEN dp.dernier_permis >= w.asof - INTERVAL '2 years'  THEN '<2a'
512	                WHEN dp.dernier_permis >= w.asof - INTERVAL '5 years'  THEN '2-5a'
513	                WHEN dp.dernier_permis >= w.asof - INTERVAL '10 years' THEN '5-10a'
514	                ELSE '10a+'
515	            END                                                                 AS permis_bin,
516	            st.canopee_pct, st.ndvi_moyen, st.friche, st.piscine, st.pv_candidat
517	        FROM p_model_frame f
518	        CROSS JOIN win w
519	        JOIN stock sk ON sk.secteur = f.secteur
520	        JOIN p_model_static st ON st.idu = f.idu
521	        LEFT JOIN z_sect zs ON zs.secteur = f.secteur AND zs.annee = w.annee
